Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any quantum_mechanics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 959. The projective resolution has length at most 378 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 378. QED.
